# Textures (TIM lists, generated)

Generated by `tools/dump_tims.py`. Rects are VRAM halfword coordinates (x y w h); an image's pixel width is w*4 (4bpp) / w*2 (8bpp). Compressed = RLE variants 0x10010 / 0x20010.

Each area's load list uploads its TIM members into VRAM (postprocess 1); `tools/vram.py` rebuilds that VRAM and renders any (TPAGE, CLUT) pair as a 256x256 texture (TPAGE bits 0-3 x/64, bit 4 y/256, bits 7-8 mode; CLUT bits 0-5 x/16, bits 6-14 y). Terrain: TPAGE 0x18 + CLUT row 0xF0+tile (notes/12). Models / meshes carry their own tpage and clut per primitive (notes/11). Actor-model textures come from other load lists (not covered here yet).
